13. (1) $\because \;S = {2R}\cos \theta  \cdot  R\sin \theta  = {R}^{2}\sin {2\theta } \leq  {R}^{2}$ ,

$\therefore$ 当 ${2\theta } = \frac{\pi }{2}$ ,即 $\theta  = \frac{\pi }{4}$ 时, ${S}_{\max } = {R}^{2}$ .

(2) $\theta  \in  \left( {0,\frac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ ，

$$
{S}_{\text{ 焦 }} = {\left( \sqrt{2}R\cos \theta \right) }^{2} = 2{R}^{2}{\cos }^{2}\theta ,
$$

$$
V = {S}_{\text{ 焦 }} \cdot  \left( {AB}\right)  = 2{R}^{2}{\cos }^{2}\theta  \cdot  R\sin \theta  = 2{R}^{3}{\cos }^{2}\theta  \cdot  \sin \theta .
$$

$$
\because \;{\left\lbrack  2{\left( {\cos }^{2}\theta \sin \theta \right) }^{2}\right\rbrack  }^{1/3} = {\left( {\cos }^{2}\theta  \cdot  {\cos }^{2}\theta  \cdot  2{\sin }^{2}\theta \right) }^{1/3}
$$

$$
\leq  \frac{1}{3}\left\lbrack  {{\cos }^{2}\theta  + {\cos }^{2}\theta  + 2{\sin }^{2}\theta }\right\rbrack   = \frac{2}{3},
$$

$$
\therefore \;2{\left( {\cos }^{2}\theta  \cdot  \sin \theta \right) }^{2} \leq  {\left( \frac{2}{3}\right) }^{3} = \frac{8}{27}.
$$

$$
\because \;{\cos }^{2}\theta \sin \theta  \leq  {\left( \frac{4}{27}\right) }^{1/2} = \frac{2\sqrt{3}}{9},
$$

$$
V = 2{R}^{3}{\cos }^{2}\theta \sin \theta  \leq  \frac{4}{9}\sqrt{3}{R}^{3},
$$

$\therefore$ 当 ${\cos }^{2}\theta  = 2{\sin }^{2}\theta ,\theta  \in  \left( {0,\frac{\pi }{2}}\right)$ ，即 $\theta  = \operatorname{arctg}\sqrt{2}$ 时，

$$
{V}_{\max } = \frac{4}{9}\sqrt{3}{R}^{3}.
$$

14. (1) $\because \frac{1}{2}\left( {{a}^{n} + {b}^{n}}\right)  \geq  {\left\lbrack  \frac{1}{2}\left( a + b\right) \right\rbrack  }^{n},\left( {n \in  N}\right)$ ,

$$
\therefore \;{\left( {a}^{n} + {b}^{n}\right) }^{\frac{1}{n}} \geq  \frac{\sqrt[n]{2}}{2}\left( {a + b}\right) \text{ . }
$$

同理,

$$
{\left( {b}^{n} + {c}^{n}\right) }^{\frac{1}{n}} \geq  \frac{\sqrt[n]{2}}{2}\left( {b + c}\right)
$$

$$
{\left( {c}^{n} + {a}^{n}\right) }^{\frac{1}{n}} \geq  \frac{\sqrt[n]{2}}{2}\left( {c + a}\right) ,
$$

三式相加, 得

$$
{\left( {a}^{n} + {b}^{n}\right) }^{\frac{1}{n}} + {\left( {b}^{n} + {c}^{n}\right) }^{\frac{1}{n}} + {\left( {c}^{n} + {a}^{n}\right) }^{\frac{1}{n}} \geq  \sqrt[n]{2}\left( {a + b + c}\right) .
$$

(2) $\because y = {x}^{n}\left( {n \in  N}\right)$ 在 $x \in  \lbrack 0, + \infty )$ 上为下凸函数，

$\therefore \;\frac{1}{m}\left( {{a}_{2}^{n} + {a}_{3}^{n} + \cdots  + {a}_{m + 1}^{n}}\right)  \geq  {\left\lbrack  \frac{1}{m}\left( {a}_{2} + {a}_{3} + \cdots  + {a}_{m + 1}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{n},$

于是 ${\left\lbrack  {a}_{2}^{n} + {a}_{3}^{n} + \cdots  + {a}_{m + 1}^{n}\right\rbrack  }^{\frac{1}{n}} \geq  \frac{\sqrt[n]{m}}{m}\left( {{a}_{2} + {a}_{3} + \cdots  + {a}_{m + 1}}\right)$ ,

即

$$
{\left\lbrack  {S}_{m + 1}^{\left( n\right) } - {a}_{1}^{n}\right\rbrack  }^{1/n} \geq  \frac{\sqrt[n]{m}}{m}\left( {{S}_{m + 1} - {a}_{1}}\right) .
$$

同理

$$
{\left\lbrack  {S}_{m + 1}^{\left( n\right) } - {a}_{2}^{n}\right\rbrack  }^{\frac{1}{n}} \geq  \frac{\sqrt[n]{m}}{m}\left( {{S}_{m + 1} - {a}_{2}}\right) ,\cdots ,{\left\lbrack  {S}_{m + 1}^{\left( n\right) } - {a}_{m + 1}^{n}\right\rbrack  }^{\frac{1}{n}}
$$

$$
\geq  \frac{\sqrt[n]{m}}{m}\left( {{S}_{m + 1} - {a}_{m + 1}}\right)
$$

将 $\left( {m + 1}\right)$ 个式相加,得

$$
{\left\lbrack  {S}_{m + 1}^{\left( n\right) } - {a}_{1}^{n}\right\rbrack  }^{1/n} + {\left\lbrack  {S}_{m + 1}^{\left( n\right) } - {a}_{2}^{n}\right\rbrack  }^{1/n} + \cdots  + {\left\lbrack  {S}_{m + 1}^{\left( n\right) } - {a}_{m + 1}^{n}\right\rbrack  }^{1/n}
$$

$$
\geq  \frac{\sqrt[n]{m}}{m}\left\lbrack  {\left( {m + 1}\right) {S}_{m + 1} - {S}_{m + 1}}\right\rbrack   = \sqrt[n]{m} \cdot  {S}_{m + 1}.
$$

15. $1 + {a}_{1} > 0,1 + {a}_{2} > 0,\cdots ,1 + {a}_{n} > 0$ ,

$$
{\left\lbrack  \left( 1 + {a}_{1}\right) \left( 1 + {a}_{2}\right) \cdots \left( 1 + {a}_{n}\right) \right\rbrack  }^{1/n}
$$

$$
\leq  \frac{1}{n}\left\lbrack  {\left( {1 + {a}_{1}}\right)  + \left( {1 + {a}_{2}}\right)  + \cdots  + \left( {1 + {a}_{n}}\right) }\right\rbrack
$$

$$
= \frac{1}{n}\left\lbrack  {n + \left( {{a}_{1} + {a}_{2} + \cdots  + {a}_{n}}\right) }\right\rbrack
$$

$$
= \frac{1}{n}\left\lbrack  {n + {S}_{n}}\right\rbrack   = 1 + \frac{{S}_{n}}{n}.
$$

$$
\left( {1 + {a}_{1}}\right) \left( {1 + {a}_{2}}\right) \cdots \left( {1 + {a}_{n}}\right)
$$

$$
\leq  {\left( 1 + \frac{{S}_{n}}{n}\right) }^{n}
$$

$$
= 1 + {O}_{n}^{1} \cdot  \frac{{S}_{n}}{n} + {O}_{n}^{2} \cdot  {\left( \frac{{S}_{n}}{n}\right) }^{2} + \cdots  + {O}_{n}^{n}{\left( \frac{{S}_{n}}{n}\right) }^{n}
$$

$$
= 1 + \frac{n}{1!} \cdot  \frac{{S}_{n}}{n} + \frac{n\left( {n - 1}\right) }{2!} \cdot  \frac{{S}_{n}^{2}}{{n}^{2}} + \cdots
$$

$$
+ \frac{n\left( {n - 1}\right) \cdots 1}{n!} \cdot  \frac{{S}_{n}^{n}}{{n}^{n}}
$$

$$
\leq  1 + \frac{n}{1!} \cdot  \frac{{S}_{n}}{n} + \frac{{n}^{2}}{2!} \cdot  \frac{{S}_{n}^{2}}{{n}^{2}} + \cdots  + \frac{{n}^{n}}{n!} \cdot  \frac{{S}_{n}^{n}}{{n}^{n}}
$$

$$
= 1 + \frac{{S}_{n}}{1!} + \frac{{S}_{n}^{2}}{2!} + \cdots  + \frac{{S}_{n}^{n}}{n!}.
$$

16. ${C}_{n}^{1} + {C}_{n}^{2} + \cdots  + {C}_{n}^{n} > n \cdot  {2}^{\frac{1}{2}\left( {n - 1}\right) } \Leftrightarrow  {2}^{n} - 1 > n \cdot  {2}^{\frac{1}{2}\left( {n - 1}\right) }$ ,而

$$
{2}^{n} - 1 = \frac{{2}^{n} - 1}{1} = \frac{{2}^{n} - 1}{2 - 1}
$$

$$
= 1 + {2}^{1} + {2}^{2} + \cdots  + {2}^{n - 2} + {2}^{n - 1}
$$

$$
> n{\left\lbrack  1 \cdot  {2}^{1} \cdot  {2}^{2} \cdot  \cdots  \cdot  {2}^{n - 2} \cdot  {2}^{n - 1}\right) }^{\frac{1}{n}}
$$

$$
= n{\left\lbrack  {2}^{\left( 1 + 2 + \cdots  + \left( n - 1\right) \right) }\right\rbrack  }^{\frac{1}{n}}
$$

$$
= n \cdot  {\left\lbrack  {2}^{\frac{1}{2}\left( {n - 1}\right) n}\right\rbrack  }^{\frac{1}{n}} = n \cdot  {2}^{\frac{1}{2}\left( {n - 1}\right) }.
$$

17. ${\left\lbrack  {\left( 1 + \frac{x}{n}\right) }^{n}\right\rbrack  }^{\frac{1}{n + 1}} = {\left\lbrack  1 \cdot  {\left( 1 + \frac{x}{n}\right) }^{n}\right\rbrack  }^{\frac{1}{n + 1}}$

$$
\leq  \frac{1}{n + 1}\left\lbrack  {1 + \left( {1 + \frac{x}{n}}\right)  + \cdots  + \left( {1 + \frac{x}{n}}\right) }\right\rbrack
$$

$$
= \frac{1}{n + 1}\left\lbrack  {\left( {n + 1}\right)  \cdot  1 + n \cdot  \frac{x}{n}}\right\rbrack
$$

$$
= \frac{1}{n + 1}\left\lbrack  {\left( {n + 1}\right)  + x}\right\rbrack   = 1 + \frac{x}{n + 1}.
$$

## 思考题十的答案与略解

1. 证

取长度分别为 $x\text{ 、 }y\text{ 、 }z$ 的三线段 ${OA}\text{ 、 }{OB}\text{ 、 }{OC}$ ,使

$$
\angle {AOB} = \angle {BOC} = \angle {OOA} = {120}^{ \circ  }\text{ ， }
$$

则 $\bigtriangleup  {ABC}$ 的三边长分别为

$$
{AB} = \sqrt{{x}^{2} + {y}^{2} - {2xy}\cos {120}^{ \circ  }} = \sqrt{{x}^{2} + {y}^{2} + {xy}},
$$

同理 ${BC} = \sqrt{{y}^{2} + {z}^{2} + {yz}},\;{CA} = \sqrt{{z}^{2} + {x}^{2} + {zx}}$ .

$$
{AB} + {CA} \geq  {BC},
$$

$\therefore \sqrt{{x}^{2} + {y}^{2} + {xy}} + \sqrt{{z}^{2} + {x}^{2} + {zx}} \geq  \sqrt{{y}^{2} + {z}^{2} + {yz}}$ .

2. 证

$$
\left( {a + b + c}\right) \left( {{h}_{a} + {h}_{b} + {h}_{c}}\right)  = {2S}\left( {a + b + c}\right) \left( {\frac{1}{a} + \frac{1}{b} + \frac{1}{c}}\right)
$$

$$
\geq  {18S}\sqrt[3]{abc}\sqrt[3]{\frac{1}{abc}}
$$

$$
= {18S}\text{ , }
$$

$\therefore$

$$
{h}_{a} + {h}_{b} + {h}_{c} \geq  \frac{18S}{2p} = {9r}.
$$

当且仅当 $a = b = c$ (即为正三角形) 时,等号成立.

3. 证 $\because$

$$
a < b + c,
$$

$\therefore$

$$
\frac{a}{a + b + c} < \frac{1}{2}.
$$

同理, 有

$$
\frac{b}{a + b + c} < \frac{1}{2}
$$

$$
\frac{c}{a + b + c} < \frac{1}{2}
$$

$$
\frac{{a\alpha } + {b\beta } + {c\gamma }}{a + b + c} < \frac{\alpha }{2} + \frac{\beta }{2} + \frac{\gamma }{2} = \frac{\pi }{2}.
$$

根据在一个三角形中, 大角对大边定理, 有

$$
\left( {a - b}\right) \left( {\alpha  - \beta }\right)  \geq  0,\left( {b - c}\right) \left( {\beta  - \gamma }\right)  \geq  0,\left( {c - a}\right) \left( {\gamma  - \alpha }\right)  \geq  0,
$$

$$
\therefore \;3\left( {{a\alpha } + {b\beta } + {c\gamma }}\right)  - \left( {a + b + c}\right) \left( {\alpha  + \beta  + \gamma }\right)
$$

$$
= \left( {a - b}\right) \left( {\alpha  - \beta }\right)  + \left( {b - c}\right) \left( {\beta  - \gamma }\right)  + \left( {c - a}\right) \left( {\gamma  - \alpha }\right)
$$

$$
\geq  0\text{ , }
$$

即

$$
\frac{{a\alpha } + {b\beta } + {c\gamma }}{a + b + c} \geq  \frac{\alpha  + \beta  + \gamma }{3} = \frac{\pi }{3}.
$$

当且仅当 $a = b = c$ ,即为正三角形时,等号成立.

4. 证 $\because$

$$
{AI} = r\csc \frac{A}{2},
$$

$$
{BI} = r\csc \frac{B}{2},{CI} = r\csc \frac{C}{2}.
$$

$$
\therefore {AI} + {BI} + {CI} = r\left( {\csc \frac{A}{2} + \csc \frac{B}{2} + \csc \frac{C}{2}}\right)
$$

$$
\geq  3{r}^{3}\sqrt[3]{\frac{1}{\sin \frac{A}{2}\sin \frac{B}{2}\sin \frac{C}{2}}}
$$

$$
\left( {\because \;\sin \frac{A}{2}\sin \frac{B}{2}\sin \frac{C}{2} \leq  \frac{1}{8}}\right)
$$

$$
\geq  {6r}\text{ . }
$$

应用 Erdös-Mordell 不等式, 也可获证.

5. 证 设 $\overrightarrow{AB} = \mathbf{a},\overrightarrow{AO} = \mathbf{b},\overrightarrow{AD} = \mathbf{c}$ ,则

$$
A{O}^{2} + B{D}^{2} + A{D}^{2} + B{O}^{2} - A{B}^{2} - C{D}^{2}
$$

$$
= {\mathbf{b}}^{2} + {\left( \mathbf{c} - \mathbf{a}\right) }^{2} + {\mathbf{c}}^{2} + {\left( \mathbf{b} - \mathbf{a}\right) }^{2} - {\mathbf{a}}^{2} - {\left( \mathbf{c} - \mathbf{b}\right) }^{2}
$$

$$
= {\mathbf{b}}^{2} + {\mathbf{c}}^{2} - 2\mathbf{{ca}} + {\mathbf{a}}^{2} + {\mathbf{c}}^{2} + {\mathbf{b}}^{2} - 2\mathbf{{ab}} + {\mathbf{a}}^{2}
$$

$$
- {\mathbf{a}}^{2} - {\mathbf{c}}^{2} + 2\mathbf{b}\mathbf{c} - {\mathbf{b}}^{2}
$$

$$
= {\mathbf{a}}^{2} + {\mathbf{b}}^{2} + {\mathbf{c}}^{2} - 2\mathbf{{ab}} + 2\mathbf{{bc}} - 2\mathbf{{ca}}
$$

$$
= {\left( \mathbf{a} - \mathbf{b} - \mathbf{c}\right) }^{2} \geq  0.
$$

6. 解 仿例 7, $m \in  \left\lbrack  {-\frac{1}{2},\frac{7}{2}}\right\rbrack$ .

7. 解 集合 $A$ 为直线 $y = {ax} + b$ 上横坐标为整数的点,集合 $B$ 为抛物线 $y = 3{x}^{2} + {15}$ 上的全部整点.

$$
\because \;A \cap  B \neq  \varnothing ,
$$

$\therefore$ 方程 $3{x}^{2} + {15} = {ax} + b$ ，

即

$$
3{x}^{2} - {ax} + {15} - b = 0
$$

有整数解. $\therefore \Delta  = {a}^{2} - {12}\left( {{15} - b}\right)  \geq  0$ ,即

$$
{a}^{2} + {12b} - {180} \geq  0
$$

①

$$
\because \;\left( {a, b}\right)  \in  O\text{ , }
$$

$$
\therefore \;{a}^{2} + {b}^{2} - {144} \leq  0
$$

②

1 ② 两异向不等式相减得

$$
{b}^{2} - {12b} + {36} < 0,
$$

即

$$
{\left( b - 6\right) }^{2} \leq  0.
$$

但

$$
{\left( b - 6\right) }^{2} \geq  0,
$$

$\therefore$

$$
b = 6\text{ . }
$$

分别代入 ①,② 得

$$
{a}^{2} + {72} - {180} \geq  0,
$$

即

$$
{a}^{2} \geq  {108}
$$

又

$$
{a}^{2} + {36} \leq  {144},
$$

即

$$
{a}^{2} \leq  {108}\text{ . }
$$

$\therefore$

$$
{a}^{2} = {108},
$$

即

$$
a =  \pm  6\sqrt{3}.
$$

方程

$$
3{x}^{2} \mp  6\sqrt{3}x + 9 = 0,
$$

即

$$
{x}^{2} \mp  2\sqrt{3}x + 3 = 0,\therefore x =  \mp  \sqrt{3}\text{ . }
$$

这与方程 $3{x}^{2} - {ax} + {15} - b = 0$ 有整数解矛盾. 故同时满足条件(1)、(2)的实数 $a$ 、 $b$ 不存在.

8. 证 设二次曲线方程为

$$
f\left( {x, y}\right)  = A{x}^{2} + {Bxy} + C{y}^{2} + {Dx} + {Ey} + F = 0
$$

①

直线 $\overrightarrow{{P}_{0}{QR}}$ 与 $\overrightarrow{{P}_{0}{ST}}$ 的倾角分别为 ${\theta }_{1}\text{ 、 }{\theta }_{2}$ ,则过点 ${P}_{0}\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)$ 的直线 $\overline{{P}_{0}{QR}}$ 与 $\overline{{P}_{0}{ST}}$ 的参数方程分别为

$$
\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = {x}_{0} + t\cos {\theta }_{1}, \\  y = {y}_{0} + t\sin {\theta }_{1}; \end{array}\right.
$$

②

$$
\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = {x}_{0} + t\cos {\theta }_{2}, \\  y = {y}_{0} + t\sin {\theta }_{2}; \end{array}\right.
$$

③

② 代入 ①, 得

$$
\left( {A{\cos }^{2}{\theta }_{1} + B\sin {\theta }_{1}\cos {\theta }_{1} + C{\sin }^{2}{\theta }_{1}}\right) {t}^{2}
$$

$$
+ \left\lbrack  {\left( {{2A}{x}_{0} + B{y}_{0} + D}\right) \cos {\theta }_{1} + \left( {B{x}_{0} + {2C}{y}_{0} + E}\right) \sin {\theta }_{1}}\right\rbrack  t
$$

$$
+ f\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right)  = 0,
$$

其两根

$$
{t}_{1} = {P}_{0}Q,{t}_{2} = {P}_{0}R.
$$

因二次项系数不等于零, 故

$$
{P}_{0}Q \cdot  {P}_{0}R = \frac{f\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right) }{A{\cos }^{2}{\theta }_{1} + B\sin {\theta }_{1}\cos {\theta }_{1} + C{\sin }^{2}{\theta }_{1}}.
$$

同理,

$$
{P}_{0}S \cdot  {P}_{0}T = \frac{f\left( {{x}_{0},{y}_{0}}\right) }{A{\cos }^{2}{\theta }_{2} + B\sin {\theta }_{2}\cos {\theta }_{2} + C{\sin }^{2}{\theta }_{2}}.
$$

$\therefore \left| \frac{{P}_{0}Q \cdot  {P}_{0}R}{{P}_{0}S \cdot  {P}_{0}T}\right|  = \left| \frac{A{\cos }^{2}{\theta }_{2} + B\sin {\theta }_{2}\cos {\theta }_{2} + C{\sin }^{2}{\theta }_{3}}{A{\cos }^{2}{\theta }_{1} + B\sin {\theta }_{1}\cos {\theta }_{1} + C{\sin }^{2}{\theta }_{1}}\right|$ ,

此为与点 ${P}_{0}$ 的位置无关的定值.

9. 证 设 $\angle {PO}{O}_{2} = \theta$ ,则

$$
\angle {AOP} = \frac{2\pi }{3} + \theta ,\angle {POC} = \frac{2\pi }{3} - \theta .
$$

令

$$
{OA} = R,{O}_{1}A = {O}_{2}B = {O}_{3}C = r,
$$

$$
\text{ J }P{O}_{1}^{2} = {R}^{2} + {\left( R - r\right) }^{2} - {2R}\left( {R - r}\right) \cos \left( {\frac{2\pi }{3} + \theta }\right) \text{ , }
$$

$$
P{L}^{2} = P{O}_{1}^{2} - {r}^{2}
$$

$$
= 2{R}^{2} - {2Rr} - {2R}\left( {R - r}\right) \cos \left( {\frac{2\pi }{3} + \theta }\right)
$$

$$
= {2R}\left( {R - r}\right) \left\lbrack  {1 - \cos \left( {\frac{2\pi }{3} + \theta }\right) }\right\rbrack
$$

$$
= {4R}\left( {R - r}\right) {\sin }^{2}\left( {\frac{\pi }{3} + \frac{\theta }{2}}\right) .
$$

同理, 有

$$
P{M}^{2} = {4R}\left( {R - r}\right) {\sin }^{2}\frac{\theta }{2},
$$

$$
P{N}^{2} = {4R}\left( {R - r}\right) {\sin }^{2}\left( {\frac{\pi }{3} - \frac{\theta }{2}}\right) .
$$

$\because \;\theta  \in  \left\lbrack  {0,\frac{2\pi }{3}}\right\rbrack  ,\;\therefore \;{PL} = 2\sqrt{R\left( {R - r}\right) }\sin \left( {\frac{\pi }{3} + \frac{\theta }{2}}\right) ,$

$$
{PM} = 2\sqrt{R\left( {R - r}\right) }\sin \frac{\theta }{2}
$$

$$
{PN} = 2\sqrt{R\left( {R - r}\right) }\sin \left( {\frac{\pi }{3} - \frac{\theta }{2}}\right) .
$$

$$
\therefore \;{PM} + {PN} = 2\sqrt{R\left( {R - r}\right) }\left\lbrack  {\sin \frac{\theta }{2} + \sin \left( {\frac{\pi }{3} - \frac{\theta }{2}}\right) }\right\rbrack
$$

$$
= 4\sqrt{R\left( {R - r}\right) }\sin \frac{\pi }{6}\cos \left( {\frac{\pi }{6} - \frac{\theta }{2}}\right)
$$

$$
= 2\sqrt{R\left( {R - r}\right) }\sin \left( {\frac{\pi }{2} - \frac{\pi }{6} + \frac{\theta }{2}}\right)
$$

$$
= 2\sqrt{R\left( {R - r}\right) }\sin \left( {\frac{\pi }{3} + \frac{\theta }{2}}\right)  = {PL}.
$$

图 3-4-第 9 题

10. 证 从例 8 知 ${\operatorname{tg}}^{2}\frac{\pi }{7},{\operatorname{tg}}^{2}\frac{3\pi }{7},{\operatorname{tg}}^{2}\frac{5\pi }{7}$ 是方程

$$
{y}^{3} - {21}{y}^{2} + {35y} - 7 = 0
$$

的根,故 ${\operatorname{tg}}^{2}\frac{\pi }{7} + {\operatorname{tg}}^{2}\frac{3\pi }{7} + {\operatorname{tg}}^{2}\frac{5\pi }{7} = {21}$ ,

$$
{\sec }^{2}\frac{\pi }{7} + {\sec }^{2}\frac{3\pi }{7} + {\sec }^{2}\frac{5\pi }{7}
$$

$$
= 1 + {\operatorname{tg}}^{2}\frac{\pi }{7} + 1 + {\operatorname{tg}}^{2}\frac{3\pi }{7} + 1 + {\operatorname{tg}}^{2}\frac{5\pi }{7} = {24}.
$$

![bo_d6seg9491nqc73ejl5d0_339_946_665_431_512_0.jpg](images/bo_d6seg9491nqc73ejl5d0_339_946_665_431_512_0.jpg)

图 3-4-第 12 题

11. 证 必要性: 如果 $\bigtriangleup {ABC}$ 是等腰三角形,则 $A = B,\therefore$ 行列式中第一行与第二行完全相同, 故

$$
\Delta  = 0\text{ . }
$$

充分性: 如果 $\Delta  = 0$ ,则三点 $\left( {{\cos }^{2}A,\sin A}\right) \text{ 、 }\left( {{\cos }^{2}B,\sin B}\right)$  、 $\left( {{\cos }^{2}C,\sin C}\right)$ 共线,但此三点在曲线 $\left\{  \begin{array}{l} x = {\cos }^{2}\theta \\  y = \sin \theta  \end{array}\right.$ 上,此曲线为抛物线 ${y}^{2} =  - x + 1\left( {x \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ 的一段弧,如图所示. 因为直线与抛物线至多只有两个交点,所以此三点中至少有两点重合, 不失一般性. 若

$$
\left( {{\cos }^{2}A,\sin A}\right)  = \left( {{\cos }^{2}B,\sin B}\right) ,
$$

$$
\left\{  \begin{array}{l} {\cos }^{2}A = {\cos }^{2}B \\  \sin A = \sin B \end{array}\right.
$$

①

②

其中 $A\text{ 、 }B \in  \left( {0,\pi }\right)$ ,从 ① 得 ${\sin }^{2}A = {\sin }^{2}B$ ,故

$$
\sin A = \sin B.
$$

$\because O < A, B < \pi$ ,且 $A + B \neq  \pi ,\;\therefore A = B$ ,即 $\bigtriangleup {ABC}$ 为等腰三角形.

## 出版 说 明

《中学数学思维方法》一书，是以上海师范大学教育科学研究所兼职研究员陈振宣同志的论文 “数学思维方法的探索” 为框架， 组织了所内外部分数学教学骨干写成的. 其中第二章 “方程观点”、“因果观点”、“递推观点”分别由陈永箴、袁霞如、蒋通淼同志执笔. 第三章 “逻辑划分” 由陈天柱同志执笔，“等价与非等价转化”、“类比与归纳”由刘继祖、王景梅同志执笔. 其他各章、节均为陈振宣同志撰写，全书由陈振宣同志统稿审定.

关于数学思维方法的研究, 还是起步不久的课题. 陈振宣同志作了 8 年的摸索, 曾在全国 20 多个城市作过专题报告, 引起了广大教研员与教师的兴趣，逐步形成了一支研究队伍. 大家都希望陈振宣同志研究、摸索得到的经验早日成书，以推动中学数学思维方法的研究, 为中学数学教学改革添砖加瓦.

本书稿曾蒙赵善继副教授精心审阅，提出了不少宝贵意见，并承吴绍云、王初宪、陈永莉三同志对思考题进行复核，在此表示衷心的感谢. 由于时间仓促, 疏漏难免, 恳请专家与读者批评指正.

1987 年 8 月
